    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Each bow subregion midpoint must be one of the four equal-subdivision midpoints derived from bowLength." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bowLength ?blq ;
                      nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?case .
                ?blq qudt:numericValue ?bl .
                ?case nltl:bowSubregionMidLengthPosition ?mpq .
                ?mpq qudt:numericValue ?mp .
                FILTER (
                    ?mp != (?bl / 8) &&
                    ?mp != (3 * ?bl / 8) &&
                    ?mp != (5 * ?bl / 8) &&
                    ?mp != (7 * ?bl / 8)
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The four bow subregion cases must have distinct midpoint positions, establishing one-to-one pairing with the four equal subdivisions." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?case1 ;
                      nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?case2 .
                FILTER (?case1 != ?case2)
                ?case1 nltl:bowSubregionMidLengthPosition ?mpq1 .
                ?case2 nltl:bowSubregionMidLengthPosition ?mpq2 .
                ?mpq1 qudt:numericValue ?mp1 .
                ?mpq2 qudt:numericValue ?mp2 .
                FILTER (?mp1 = ?mp2)
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "selectedMaximumBowForce must be at least every reported bow subregion force and must equal the expected maximum within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 with a small absolute fallback." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:selectedMaximumBowForce ?sq ;
                      nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?case .
                ?sq qudt:numericValue ?sv .
                ?case nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedForce ?fq .
                ?fq qudt:numericValue ?fv .
                {
                    SELECT $this (MAX(?v) AS ?expected)
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?c .
                        ?c nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedForce ?q .
                        ?q qudt:numericValue ?v .
                    }
                    GROUP BY $this
                }
                FILTER (
                    ?sv < ?fv ||
                    ABS(?sv - ?expected) >
                    IF(ABS(?expected) > "0.001"^^xsd:decimal,
                       "0.000001"^^xsd:decimal * ABS(?expected),
                       "0.000001"^^xsd:decimal)
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;

--- PYSHACL REPORT ---
The graph now preserves the prior midpoint, one-to-one pairing, unrestricted evidence cardinality, and tolerance/max-selection repairs, and the nested aggregate SELECT includes $this. However, the DIRECT_CALCULATION SPARQL uses ABS(), which is outside the permitted calculation operators. Replace each ABS-based difference and magnitude test with supported arithmetic and simple piecewise comparisons (or an equivalent bounded formulation using only +, -, *, /, comparisons, min/max, and fixed constants), while retaining tolerance scaling from the expected maximum and the >= comparison against every reported case value.

            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?c .

                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?c .

            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?c .
                        ?c nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedForce ?q .

PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),

    AS ?basicValid)

    (?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree &&
     ?requiredRank = 0 &&
     ((?t > 35 && ?t <= 45 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3)) ||

    AS ?notApplicable)

  FILTER (
    !?basicValid ||
    (?requiredRank = 0 && !?notApplicable) ||
    (?notApplicable && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?unexpected
    }) ||

      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      FILTER(?required = ?requiredGrade)

      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      ?required nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?requiredActualRank
      FILTER(?actualRank < ?requiredActualRank)
    })
  )
}

PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
  $this nltl:asBuiltPlateThickness ?q ;
        nltl:steelMaterialClass ?mc ;
        nltl:steelStrengthCategory ?sc ;
        nltl:actualHullStructuralSteelGrade ?actual .
  ?q qudt:numericValue ?t ;
     qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> .
  ?actual nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?actualRank .

  BIND (
    IF(?t > 0 && ?t <= 10,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
          IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) &&
             ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
    IF(?t > 10 && ?t <= 15,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, 1,
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
             IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0))),
    IF(?t > 15 && ?t <= 25,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0)),
    IF(?t > 25 && ?t <= 35,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne,
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
          IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree),
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0)),
    IF(?t > 35 && ?t <= 40,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, 2,
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
             IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0)), 0))), 
    IF(?t > 40 && ?t <= 45,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 4,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0))), 0)), 
    IF(?t > 45 && ?t <= 50,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 4,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0))), 0)), 0)), 0)), 0)), 0)))))) 
    AS ?requiredRank)

  BIND (
    IF(?sc = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory,
       IF(?requiredRank = 1, nltl:steelGradeB,
          IF(?requiredRank = 2, nltl:steelGradeD,
             IF(?requiredRank = 3, nltl:steelGradeE, nltl:steelGradeValue))),
       IF(?requiredRank = 1, nltl:steelGradeAh,
          IF(?requiredRank = 2, nltl:steelGradeDh,
             IF(?requiredRank = 3, nltl:steelGradeEh,
                IF(?requiredRank = 4, nltl:steelGradeFh, nltl:steelGradeValue)))))
    AS ?requiredGrade)

  BIND (
    (?t > 0 && ?t <= 50 &&
     ?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) &&
     ?sc IN (nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory, nltl:highTensileSteelCategory) &&
     ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7))
    AS ?basicValid)

  BIND (
    (?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree &&
     ?requiredRank = 0 &&
     ((?t > 35 && ?t <= 45 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3)) ||
      (?t > 45 && ?t <= 50 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5))))
    AS ?notApplicable)

  FILTER (
    !?basicValid ||
    (?requiredRank = 0 && !?notApplicable) ||
    (?notApplicable && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?unexpected
    }) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && !(EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      FILTER(?required = ?requiredGrade)
    })) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      ?required nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?requiredActualRank
      FILTER(?actualRank < ?requiredActualRank)
    })
  )
}
"""
    ] .

    AS ?basicValid)

  BIND (
    (?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree &&
     ?requiredRank = 0 &&
     ((?t > 35 && ?t <= 45 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3)) ||
      (?t > 45 && ?t <= 50 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5))))
    AS ?notApplicable)

  FILTER (
    !?basicValid ||
    (?requiredRank = 0 && !?notApplicable) ||
    (?notApplicable && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?unexpected
    }) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && !(EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      FILTER(?required = ?requiredGrade)
    })) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      ?required nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?requiredActualRank
      FILTER(?actualRank < ?requiredActualRank)
    })
  )
}
"""
    ] .

PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
  $this nltl:asBuiltPlateThickness ?q ;
        nltl:steelMaterialClass ?mc ;
        nltl:steelStrengthCategory ?sc ;
        nltl:actualHullStructuralSteelGrade ?actual .
  ?q qudt:numericValue ?t ;
     qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> .
  ?actual nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?actualRank .

  BIND (
    IF(?t > 0 && ?t <= 10,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
          IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) &&
             ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
    IF(?t > 10 && ?t <= 15,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, 1,
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
             IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0))),
    IF(?t > 15 && ?t <= 25,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0)),
    IF(?t > 25 && ?t <= 35,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne,
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
          IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree),
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0)),
    IF(?t > 35 && ?t <= 40,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, 2,
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
             IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0)), 0))), 
    IF(?t > 40 && ?t <= 45,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 4,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0))), 0)), 
    IF(?t > 45 && ?t <= 50,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 4,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0))), 0)), 0)), 0)), 0)), 0)))))) 
    AS ?requiredRank)

  BIND (
    IF(?sc = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory,
       IF(?requiredRank = 1, nltl:steelGradeB,
          IF(?requiredRank = 2, nltl:steelGradeD,
             IF(?requiredRank = 3, nltl:steelGradeE, nltl:steelGradeValue))),
       IF(?requiredRank = 1, nltl:steelGradeAh,
          IF(?requiredRank = 2, nltl:steelGradeDh,
             IF(?requiredRank = 3, nltl:steelGradeEh,
                IF(?requiredRank = 4, nltl:steelGradeFh, nltl:steelGradeValue)))))
    AS ?requiredGrade)

  BIND (
    (?t > 0 && ?t <= 50 &&
     ?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) &&
     ?sc IN (nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory, nltl:highTensileSteelCategory) &&
     ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7))
    AS ?basicValid)

  BIND (
    (?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree &&
     ?requiredRank = 0 &&
     ((?t > 35 && ?t <= 45 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3)) ||
      (?t > 45 && ?t <= 50 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5))))
    AS ?notApplicable)

  FILTER (
    !?basicValid ||
    (?requiredRank = 0 && !?notApplicable) ||
    (?notApplicable && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?unexpected
    }) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && !(EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      FILTER(?required = ?requiredGrade)
    })) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      ?required nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?requiredActualRank
      FILTER(?actualRank < ?requiredActualRank)
    })
  )
}
"""

  ?q qudt:numericValue ?t ;
     qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> .
  ?actual nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?actualRank .

  BIND (
    IF(?t > 0 && ?t <= 10,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
          IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) &&
             ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
    IF(?t > 10 && ?t <= 15,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, 1,
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
             IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0))),
    IF(?t > 15 && ?t <= 25,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0)),
    IF(?t > 25 && ?t <= 35,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne,
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
          IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree),
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0)),
    IF(?t > 35 && ?t <= 40,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, 2,
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
             IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0)), 0))), 
    IF(?t > 40 && ?t <= 45,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 4,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0))), 0)), 
    IF(?t > 45 && ?t <= 50,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 4,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0))), 0)), 0)), 0)), 0)), 0)))))) 
    AS ?requiredRank)

  BIND (
    IF(?sc = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory,
       IF(?requiredRank = 1, nltl:steelGradeB,
          IF(?requiredRank = 2, nltl:steelGradeD,
             IF(?requiredRank = 3, nltl:steelGradeE, nltl:steelGradeValue))),
       IF(?requiredRank = 1, nltl:steelGradeAh,
          IF(?requiredRank = 2, nltl:steelGradeDh,
             IF(?requiredRank = 3, nltl:steelGradeEh,
                IF(?requiredRank = 4, nltl:steelGradeFh, nltl:steelGradeValue)))))
    AS ?requiredGrade)

  BIND (
    (?t > 0 && ?t <= 50 &&
     ?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) &&
     ?sc IN (nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory, nltl:highTensileSteelCategory) &&
     ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7))
    AS ?basicValid)

  BIND (
    (?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree &&
     ?requiredRank = 0 &&
     ((?t > 35 && ?t <= 45 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3)) ||
      (?t > 45 && ?t <= 50 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5))))
    AS ?notApplicable)

  FILTER (
    !?basicValid ||
    (?requiredRank = 0 && !?notApplicable) ||
    (?notApplicable && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?unexpected
    }) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && !(EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      FILTER(?required = ?requiredGrade)
    })) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      ?required nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?requiredActualRank
      FILTER(?actualRank < ?requiredActualRank)
    })
  )
}
"""
    ] .

PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
  $this nltl:asBuiltPlateThickness ?q ;
        nltl:steelMaterialClass ?mc ;
        nltl:steelStrengthCategory ?sc ;
        nltl:actualHullStructuralSteelGrade ?actual .
  ?q qudt:numericValue ?t ;
     qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> .
  ?actual nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?actualRank .

  BIND (
    IF(?t > 0 && ?t <= 10,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
          IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) &&
             ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
    IF(?t > 10 && ?t <= 15,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, 1,
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
             IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0))),
    IF(?t > 15 && ?t <= 25,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),

                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0)),
    IF(?t > 25 && ?t <= 35,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne,
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 2,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 1, 0)),
          IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree),
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0)),
    IF(?t > 35 && ?t <= 40,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, 2,
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
             IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0)), 0))), 
    IF(?t > 40 && ?t <= 45,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,

                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0))), 0)), 
    IF(?t > 45 && ?t <= 50,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 4,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0))), 0)), 0)), 0)), 0)), 0)))))) 
    AS ?requiredRank)

  BIND (
    IF(?sc = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory,
       IF(?requiredRank = 1, nltl:steelGradeB,
          IF(?requiredRank = 2, nltl:steelGradeD,
             IF(?requiredRank = 3, nltl:steelGradeE, nltl:steelGradeValue))),
       IF(?requiredRank = 1, nltl:steelGradeAh,
          IF(?requiredRank = 2, nltl:steelGradeDh,
             IF(?requiredRank = 3, nltl:steelGradeEh,
                IF(?requiredRank = 4, nltl:steelGradeFh, nltl:steelGradeValue)))))
    AS ?requiredGrade)

  BIND (
    (?t > 0 && ?t <= 50 &&
     ?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) &&
     ?sc IN (nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory, nltl:highTensileSteelCategory) &&
     ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7))
    AS ?basicValid)

  BIND (
    (?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree &&
     ?requiredRank = 0 &&
     ((?t > 35 && ?t <= 45 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3)) ||
      (?t > 45 && ?t <= 50 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5))))
    AS ?notApplicable)

  FILTER (
    !?basicValid ||
    (?requiredRank = 0 && !?notApplicable) ||
    (?notApplicable && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?unexpected
    }) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && !(EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      FILTER(?required = ?requiredGrade)
    })) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      ?required nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?requiredActualRank
      FILTER(?actualRank < ?requiredActualRank)
    })
  )
}

             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)), 0)),
    IF(?t > 35 && ?t <= 40,
       IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, 2,
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
             IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0)), 0))), 
    IF(?t > 40 && ?t <= 45,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 4,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0))), 0)), 
    IF(?t > 45 && ?t <= 50,
       IF(?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo),
          IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 3,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 2, 0)),
          IF(?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree,
             IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3), 0,
                IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5), 4,
                   IF(?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7), 3, 0))), 0)), 0)), 0)), 0)), 0)))))) 
    AS ?requiredRank)

  BIND (
    IF(?sc = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory,
       IF(?requiredRank = 1, nltl:steelGradeB,
          IF(?requiredRank = 2, nltl:steelGradeD,
             IF(?requiredRank = 3, nltl:steelGradeE, nltl:steelGradeValue))),
       IF(?requiredRank = 1, nltl:steelGradeAh,
          IF(?requiredRank = 2, nltl:steelGradeDh,
             IF(?requiredRank = 3, nltl:steelGradeEh,
                IF(?requiredRank = 4, nltl:steelGradeFh, nltl:steelGradeValue)))))
    AS ?requiredGrade)

  BIND (
    (?t > 0 && ?t <= 50 &&
     ?mc IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) &&
     ?sc IN (nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory, nltl:highTensileSteelCategory) &&
     ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5, nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7))
    AS ?basicValid)

  BIND (
    (?mc = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree &&
     ?requiredRank = 0 &&
     ((?t > 35 && ?t <= 45 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3)) ||
      (?t > 45 && ?t <= 50 && ?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5))))
    AS ?notApplicable)

  FILTER (
    !?basicValid ||
    (?requiredRank = 0 && !?notApplicable) ||
    (?notApplicable && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?unexpected
    }) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && !(EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      FILTER(?required = ?requiredGrade)
    })) ||
    (?requiredRank > 0 && EXISTS {
      $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required
      ?required nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?requiredActualRank
      FILTER(?actualRank < ?requiredActualRank)
    })
  )
}
"""
    ] .

gen:I2_014_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bowSubregionCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:minInclusive 4 ;
        sh:maxInclusive 4 ;
        sh:message "The bow region must be divided into four sub-regions."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ;
        sh:minCount 4 ;
        sh:maxCount 4 ;
        sh:node gen:I2_014_BowSubregionCaseShape ;
        sh:message "Four bow subregion calculation cases, each with the required calculation evidence and reported values, are required."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:selectedMaximumBowForce ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:I2_014_ForceQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "A selected maximum bow force is required."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:selectedMaximumBowLineLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:I2_014_LineLoadQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "A selected maximum bow line load is required."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:selectedMaximumBowPressure ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:I2_014_PressureQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "A selected maximum bow pressure is required."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The selected maximum bow force, line load, and pressure must equal the respective maxima reported for the four bow subregion calculation cases." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this
                    nltl:selectedMaximumBowForce ?selectedForceNode ;
                    nltl:selectedMaximumBowLineLoad ?selectedLineLoadNode ;
                    nltl:selectedMaximumBowPressure ?selectedPressureNode .

                ?selectedForceNode qudt:numericValue ?selectedForce .
                ?selectedLineLoadNode qudt:numericValue ?selectedLineLoad .
                ?selectedPressureNode qudt:numericValue ?selectedPressure .

                FILTER (
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?forceCase .
                        ?forceCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedForce ?forceNode .
                        ?forceNode qudt:numericValue ?forceValue .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?greaterForceCase .
                            ?greaterForceCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedForce ?greaterForceNode .
                            ?greaterForceNode qudt:numericValue ?greaterForce .
                            FILTER (?greaterForce > ?forceValue)
                        }
                        FILTER (?selectedForce != ?forceValue)
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?lineLoadCase .
                        ?lineLoadCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedLineLoad ?lineLoadNode .
                        ?lineLoadNode qudt:numericValue ?lineLoadValue .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?greaterLineLoadCase .
                            ?greaterLineLoadCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedLineLoad ?greaterLineLoadNode .
                            ?greaterLineLoadNode qudt:numericValue ?greaterLineLoad .
                            FILTER (?greaterLineLoad > ?lineLoadValue)
                        }
                        FILTER (?selectedLineLoad != ?lineLoadValue)
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?pressureCase .
                        ?pressureCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedPressure ?pressureNode .
                        ?pressureNode qudt:numericValue ?pressureValue .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?greaterPressureCase .
                            ?greaterPressureCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedPressure ?greaterPressureNode .
                            ?greaterPressureNode qudt:numericValue ?greaterPressure .
                            FILTER (?greaterPressure > ?pressureValue)
                        }
                        FILTER (?selectedPressure != ?pressureValue)
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5)) AS ?pc45)
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7)) AS ?pc67)

                BIND(
                    IF(
                        (
                            ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                            (
                                (?band = 1 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||

                            )
                        ),
                        nltl:steelGradeB,
                        IF(
                            (
                                ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                                (
                                    (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                    (?band = 9 && ?pc67 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo))
                                )
                            ),
                            nltl:steelGradeD,
                            IF(
                                (
                                    ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                                    (
                                        (?band = 1 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67) ||
                                        (?band = 9 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band = 9 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67)
                                    )
                                ),
                                nltl:steelGradeE,
                                IF(
                                    (
                                        ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                        (
                                            (?band >= 1 && ?band <= 4 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                            (?band = 1 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67) ||
                                            (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 9 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) && ?pc67)
                                        )
                                    ),
                                    nltl:steelGradeAh,
                                    IF(
                                        (
                                            ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                            (
                                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) ||
                                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne) ||
                                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                                (?band = 9 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo))
                                            )
                                        ),
                                        nltl:steelGradeDh,
                                        IF(

                                                ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&

        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The required grade must be the Table 8 grade selected by Polar Class, thickness, material class and strength category; the actual grade must be at least that grade within the same strength category, and non-applicable combinations must have no required grade." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                ?ship a nltl:ship ;
                      nltl:polarClass ?pc ;
                      nltl:hasSteelGradeRequirementCase $this .
                $this nltl:asBuiltPlateThickness ?thicknessNode ;
                      nltl:steelMaterialClass ?material ;

                    IF(?thickness <= 15, 2,
                    IF(?thickness <= 20, 3,
                    IF(?thickness <= 25, 4,
                    IF(?thickness <= 30, 5,
                    IF(?thickness <= 35, 6,

                    IF(?thickness <= 45, 8,

                )

                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5)) AS ?pc45)
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7)) AS ?pc67)

                BIND(

                        (
                            ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                            (
                                (?band = 1 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc67 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||

                            )
                        ),
                        nltl:steelGradeB,
                        IF(
                            (
                                ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                                (
                                    (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||

                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||

                                        (?band = 9 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67)
                                    )
                                ),
                                nltl:steelGradeE,
                                IF(
                                    (
                                        ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                        (
                                            (?band >= 1 && ?band <= 4 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                            (?band = 1 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67) ||
                                            (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 9 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) && ?pc67)
                                        )
                                    ),
                                    nltl:steelGradeAh,
                                    IF(
                                        (
                                            ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                            (
                                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) ||
                                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne) ||
                                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                                (?band = 9 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo))
                                            )
                                        ),
                                        nltl:steelGradeDh,
                                        IF(
                                            (
                                                ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&

                                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) ||

        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                ?ship a nltl:ship ;
                      nltl:polarClass ?pc ;
                      nltl:hasSteelGradeRequirementCase $this .
                $this nltl:asBuiltPlateThickness ?thicknessNode ;
                      nltl:steelMaterialClass ?material ;
                      nltl:steelStrengthCategory ?strength ;
                      nltl:actualHullStructuralSteelGrade ?actual .
                ?thicknessNode qudt:numericValue ?thickness .
                BIND(
                    IF(?thickness <= 10, 1,
                    IF(?thickness <= 15, 2,
                    IF(?thickness <= 20, 3,
                    IF(?thickness <= 25, 4,
                    IF(?thickness <= 30, 5,
                    IF(?thickness <= 35, 6,
                    IF(?thickness <= 40, 7,
                    IF(?thickness <= 45, 8,
                    IF(?thickness <= 50, 9, 0)))))))))
                    AS ?band
                )
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3)) AS ?pc13)
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5)) AS ?pc15)
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5)) AS ?pc45)
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7)) AS ?pc67)

                BIND(
                    IF(
                        (
                            ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                            (
                                (?band = 1 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc67 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 6 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne)
                            )
                        ),
                        nltl:steelGradeB,
                        IF(
                            (
                                ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                                (
                                    (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                    (?band = 9 && ?pc67 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo))
                                )
                            ),
                            nltl:steelGradeD,
                            IF(
                                (
                                    ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                                    (
                                        (?band = 1 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67) ||
                                        (?band = 9 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band = 9 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67)
                                    )
                                ),
                                nltl:steelGradeE,
                                IF(
                                    (
                                        ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                        (
                                            (?band >= 1 && ?band <= 4 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                            (?band = 1 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67) ||
                                            (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 9 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) && ?pc67)
                                        )
                                    ),
                                    nltl:steelGradeAh,
                                    IF(
                                        (
                                            ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                            (
                                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) ||
                                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne) ||
                                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                                (?band = 9 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo))
                                            )
                                        ),
                                        nltl:steelGradeDh,
                                        IF(
                                            (
                                                ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                                (
                                                    (?band >= 1 && ?band <= 4 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc15) ||
                                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo && ?pc15) ||
                                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) ||
                                                    (?band = 9 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                                    (?band = 9 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree)
                                                )
                                            ),
                                            nltl:steelGradeEh,
                                            IF(
                                                (
                                                    ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                                    ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree &&
                                                    (
                                                        (?band >= 7 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc13) ||
                                                        (?band = 9 && (?pc13 || ?pc45))
                                                    )
                                                ),
                                                nltl:steelGradeFh,
                                                nltl:benchmarkEntity
                                            )
                                        )
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    )
                    AS ?expected
                )

                OPTIONAL {
                    $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required .
                }

                OPTIONAL {
                    ?actual nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?actualRank .
                }

                FILTER(
                    ?expected = nltl:benchmarkEntity ||
                    !BOUND(?required) ||
                    ?required != ?expected ||
                    !BOUND(?actualRank) ||
                    EXISTS {

                    } ||

        sh:message "The required grade must be the Table 8 grade selected by Polar Class, thickness, material class and strength category; the actual grade must be at least that grade within the same strength category, and non-applicable combinations must have no required grade." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                ?ship a nltl:ship ;
                      nltl:polarClass ?pc ;
                      nltl:hasSteelGradeRequirementCase $this .
                $this nltl:asBuiltPlateThickness ?thicknessNode ;
                      nltl:steelMaterialClass ?material ;
                      nltl:steelStrengthCategory ?strength ;
                      nltl:actualHullStructuralSteelGrade ?actual .
                ?thicknessNode qudt:numericValue ?thickness .
                BIND(
                    IF(?thickness <= 10, 1,
                    IF(?thickness <= 15, 2,
                    IF(?thickness <= 20, 3,
                    IF(?thickness <= 25, 4,
                    IF(?thickness <= 30, 5,
                    IF(?thickness <= 35, 6,
                    IF(?thickness <= 40, 7,
                    IF(?thickness <= 45, 8,
                    IF(?thickness <= 50, 9, 0)))))))))
                    AS ?band
                )
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3)) AS ?pc13)
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc1, nltl:polarClassPc2, nltl:polarClassPc3, nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5)) AS ?pc15)
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc4, nltl:polarClassPc5)) AS ?pc45)
                BIND((?pc IN (nltl:polarClassPc6, nltl:polarClassPc7)) AS ?pc67)

                BIND(
                    IF(
                        (
                            ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                            (
                                (?band = 1 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc67 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 6 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne)
                            )
                        ),
                        nltl:steelGradeB,
                        IF(
                            (
                                ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                                (
                                    (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                    (?band = 9 && ?pc67 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo))
                                )
                            ),
                            nltl:steelGradeD,
                            IF(
                                (
                                    ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&
                                    (
                                        (?band = 1 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo, nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67) ||
                                        (?band = 9 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) && ?pc15) ||
                                        (?band = 9 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67)
                                    )
                                ),
                                nltl:steelGradeE,
                                IF(
                                    (
                                        ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                        (
                                            (?band >= 1 && ?band <= 4 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                            (?band = 1 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc67) ||
                                            (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 9 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) && ?pc67)
                                        )
                                    ),
                                    nltl:steelGradeAh,
                                    IF(
                                        (
                                            ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                            (
                                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                                (?band >= 2 && ?band <= 4 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) ||
                                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassOne) ||
                                                (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc15 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo) ||
                                                (?band = 9 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo))
                                            )
                                        ),
                                        nltl:steelGradeDh,
                                        IF(
                                            (
                                                ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                                (
                                                    (?band >= 1 && ?band <= 4 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree && ?pc15) ||
                                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo && ?pc15) ||
                                                    (?band >= 5 && ?band <= 8 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree) ||
                                                    (?band = 9 && ?pc15 && ?material IN (nltl:steelMaterialClassOne, nltl:steelMaterialClassTwo)) ||
                                                    (?band = 9 && ?pc67 && ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree)
                                                )
                                            ),
                                            nltl:steelGradeEh,
                                            IF(
                                                (
                                                    ?strength = nltl:highTensileSteelCategory &&
                                                    ?material = nltl:steelMaterialClassThree &&
                                                    (
                                                        (?band >= 7 && ?band <= 8 && ?pc13) ||
                                                        (?band = 9 && (?pc13 || ?pc45))
                                                    )
                                                ),
                                                nltl:steelGradeFh,
                                                nltl:benchmarkEntity
                                            )
                                        )
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    )
                    AS ?expected
                )

                OPTIONAL {
                    $this nltl:requiredHullStructuralSteelGrade ?required .
                }

                OPTIONAL {
                    ?actual nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?actualRank .
                }

                FILTER(
                    ?expected = nltl:benchmarkEntity ||
                    !BOUND(?required) ||
                    ?required != ?expected ||
                    !BOUND(?actualRank) ||
                    EXISTS {
                        ?required nltl:steelGradeOrderRank ?requiredRank .

                    (
                        ?strength = nltl:normalStrengthSteelCategory &&

                            nltl:steelGradeDh,
                            nltl:steelGradeEh,
                            nltl:steelGradeFh

PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:shipIceStrengthened ?ice ;
          nltl:constructionDate ?date .
    FILTER (
        ?ice = true &&
        (
            YEAR(?date) < 2026 ||
            (YEAR(?date) = 2026 && MONTH(?date) < 1) ||
            (YEAR(?date) = 2026 && MONTH(?date) = 1 && DAY(?date) < 1)
        )
    )
}
"""
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:and (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                        sh:hasValue true ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                        sh:minInclusive "2026-01-01"^^xsd:date ;
                        sh:minCount 1

                                sh:path nltl:independentEchoSoundingDeviceCount ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                                sh:minInclusive 2 ;
                                sh:minCount 1

                                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                                sh:hasValue true ;
                                sh:minCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    )
                ]
            )
        )
    ) ;
